# aspnetcore library index

ASP.NET Core documentation. Version 11, from <https://github.com/dotnet/AspNetCore.Docs> at main, commit 53d05ee9faf3cfe577c6180a0a4c6966d8396ffd.
License: CC-BY-4.0, by Microsoft and the ASP.NET Core docs contributors; see THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md. The text was converted to plain Markdown and cut into files of 16 KB at most.

This index is long: search it with grep for your words, never read it whole. Then open one file and read only the section you need.

- [Breaking change: Minimal API renames in RC 1](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/6/rc1-minimal-api-renames.md): Some APIs were renamed to improve the consistency of type names and to remove "minimal" and "action" from the API names. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Minimal API renames in RC 2](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/6/rc2-minimal-api-renames.md): To improve the consistency of type names, two classes were renamed, and one class was removed and its methods merged into the existing Microsoft.AspNetCore.Builder.EndpointRouteBuilderExtensions… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: SignalR Java Client updated to RxJava3](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/6/signalr-java-client-updated.md): The SignalR Java Client is now RxJava3. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: TryParse and BindAsync methods are validated](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/6/tryparse-bindasync-validation.md): ASP.NET Core now validates TryParse and BindAsync methods on parameter types for Map methods. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: API controller actions try to infer parameters from DI](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/api-controller-action-parameters-di.md): The mechanism to infer binding sources of API controller action parameters now marks parameters to be bound from the Dependency Injection (DI) container when… (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: AuthenticateAsync for remote auth providers](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/authenticateasync-anonymous-request.md): Remote authentication providers like OpenIdConnect, WsFederation, and OAuth have been updated to avoid unnecessary errors when there's no user information available on the request. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Default authentication scheme](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/default-authentication-scheme.md): Starting in .NET 7, we introduced new behavior in the authentication area in ASP.NET Core. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: ASPNET-prefixed environment variable precedence](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/environment-variable-precedence.md): Starting in .NET 7, and only when using the WebApplicationBuilder host, command-line arguments and DOTNET-prefixed environment variables override ASPNET-prefixed environment variables when reading from… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Fallback file endpoints](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/fallback-file-endpoints.md): The Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.ConsumesAttribute attribute allows controller actions to specify their supported content types. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Kestrel: Default HTTPS binding removed](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/https-binding-kestrel.md): The default HTTPS address and port have been removed from Kestrel in .NET 7. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: IHubClients and IHubCallerClients hide members](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/ihubclients-ihubcallerclients.md): To add support for client results, Microsoft.AspNetCore.SignalR.IHubClients and Microsoft.AspNetCore.SignalR.IHubCallerClients now hide interface members IClientProxy Client(string connectionId); and IClientProxy Caller { get; } with ISingleClientProxy… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Microsoft.AspNetCore.Server.Kestrel.Transport.Libuv and libuv.dll removed](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/libuv-transport-dll-removed.md): Microsoft.AspNetCore.Server.Kestrel.Transport.Libuv and libuv.dll have been removed. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: Event IDs for some Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Core log messages changed](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/microsoft-aspnetcore-mvc-core-log-event-ids.md): As part of updating the Microsoft.AspNetcore.Mvc.Core assembly to use Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.LoggerMessageAttribute, the ASP.NET Core team discovered logger event IDs that were reused within a single… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Microsoft.Data.SqlClient updated to 4.0.1](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/microsoft-data-sqlclient-updated-to-4-0-1.md): The Microsoft.Data.SqlClient package has been updated to 4.0.1. (1 KB)
- [Breaking change: Middleware no longer defers to endpoint with null request delegate](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/middleware-null-requestdelegate.md): As detailed in <https://github.com/dotnet/aspnetcore/issues/42413>, the file-serving middleware (DefaultFilesMiddleware, DirectoryBrowserMiddleware, and StaticFileMiddleware) have been updated to no longer no-op (that is, defer to the next… (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: MVC's detection of an empty body in model binding changed](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/mvc-empty-body-model-binding.md): The mechanism to detect an empty request body during MVC model binding now uses Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http.Features.IHttpRequestBodyDetectionFeature.CanHaveBody. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: Output caching API changes](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/output-caching-renames.md): Some APIs in the Microsoft.AspNetCore.OutputCaching namespace have changed to better represent their intent. (3 KB)
- [Breaking changes in ASP.NET Core 7](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/overview.md): If you're migrating an app to ASP.NET Core 7, the breaking changes listed here might affect you. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: SignalR Hub methods try to resolve parameters from DI](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/signalr-hub-method-parameters-di.md): SignalR Hub methods now support injecting services from your Dependency Injection (DI) container. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Authentication in WebAssembly apps](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/7/wasm-app-authentication.md): We updated the support for authentication in Blazor WebAssembly apps to rely on the history state instead of query strings in the URL. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Rate-limiting middleware requires AddRateLimiter](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/8/addratelimiter-requirement.md): ASP.NET Core rate-limiting middleware has been updated with extra functionality. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: IFormFile parameters require anti-forgery checks](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/8/antiforgery-checks.md): Minimal API endpoints that consume an Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http.IFormFile or Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http.IFormFileCollection are now opted into requiring anti-forgery token <example-value> using the new anti-forgery middleware. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: ConcurrencyLimiterMiddleware is obsolete](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/8/concurrencylimitermiddleware-obsolete.md): Microsoft.AspNetCore.ConcurrencyLimiter.ConcurrencyLimiterMiddleware and its associated methods and types have been marked as obsolete. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Forwarded headers middleware ignores X-Forwarded-* headers from unknown proxies](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/8/forwarded-headers-unknown-proxies.md): Starting in ASP.NET Core 8.0.17 and 9.0.6, the forwarded headers middleware ignores all X-Forwarded- headers from proxies that aren't explicitly configured as trusted. (4 KB)
- [Breaking change: HTTP logging middleware requires AddHttpLogging()](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/8/httplogging-addhttplogging-requirement.md): ASP.NET Core HTTP logging middleware has been updated with extra functionality. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: ISystemClock is obsolete](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/8/isystemclock-obsolete.md): Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.ISystemClock has been used by ASP.NET Core's authentication and identity components since version 1.0 to enable unit testing of time-related functionality, like expiration checking. (8 KB)
- [Breaking changes in ASP.NET Core 8](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/8/overview.md): If you're migrating an app to ASP.NET Core 8, the breaking changes listed here might affect you. (2 KB)
- [Breaking change: Custom converters for serialization removed](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/8/problemdetails-custom-converters.md): Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.ProblemDetails and Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.ValidationProblemDetails previously used custom converters to support JSON serialization due to a lack of built-in support for the IgnoreNullValues option. (3 KB)
- [Breaking change: Security token events return a JsonWebToken](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/8/securitytoken-events.md): The Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer.JwtBearerEvents, Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.WsFederation.WsFederationEvents, and Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.OpenIdConnect.OpenIdConnectEvents events are authentication events fired respectively by the JwtBearer, WsFederation, and OpenIdConnect authentication handlers. - [Legacy Mono and Emscripten JavaScript APIs not exported to global namespace in Blazor WebAssembly apps](library/aspnetcore/breaking-changes/9/legacy-apis.md): Blazor WebAssembly no longer exports legacy Mono and Emscripten APIs to the global namespace. (2 KB)
